# ADCIRC output writers — fort.6X/7X, harmonic, max-value, NetCDF

## Scope

How ADCIRC writes time-stamped output (fort.61/62/63/64), met output (fort.71-74), harmonic analysis (fort.51-53), 3D station output (fort.41-42), and max-value outputs (maxele/maxvel). Format selection (ASCII vs NetCDF), spool counters, and parallel post-merge.

## A. fort.61/63 (elevation)

- writeOutput2D의 정의는 src/write_output.F:1819에 있다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1819]` ` SUBROUTINE writeOutput2D(IT,TimeLoc) `)
- writeOutput2D는 `(it.gt.ptr(i) % descript2D % startTimeStep).and.(it.le.ptr(i) % descript2D % endTimeStep)` 조건에서 spoolCounter를 증가시킨다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1861]` ` if ( (it.gt.ptr(i) % descript2D % startTimeStep).and. `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1862]` ` &        (it.le.ptr(i) % descript2D % endTimeStep) ) then `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1863]` ` ptr(i) % descript2D % spoolCounter = `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1864]` ` &          ptr(i) % descript2D % spoolCounter + 1 `)
- spoolCounter가 outputTimeStepIncrement와 다르면 해당 출력 쓰기를 건너뛴다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1870]` ` if (ptr(i) % descript2D % spoolCounter.ne. `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1871]` ` &       ptr(i) % descript2D % outputTimeStepIncrement) then `)
- fort.61 descriptor의 outputTimeStepIncrement는 NSPOOLE에 연결된다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=524]` ` ElevStaDescript % outputTimeStepIncrement = NSPOOLE `)
- initOutput2D는 fort.61에 NOUTE를 연결한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=512]` ` ElevStaDescript % specifier            = NOUTE `)
- initOutput2D는 fort.63에 NOUTGE를 연결한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=615]` ` ElevDescript % specifier            = NOUTGE `)
- Format by `ABS(NOUTE/NOUTGE)`:
  - `1` = ASCII
  - v56.2.1은 시계열 binary 형식의 절댓값 2를 거부한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3634]` ` CASE(2) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3635]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3636]` ` &                     message='Binary format output is no longer supported.') `) 최초 변경 판이 v55인지는 확인하지 않음.
  - `3` = NetCDF3
  - `5` = NetCDF4
  - 절댓값 4는 NOUTGE·NOUTGV·NOUTGW의 sparse ASCII이다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4154]` ` CASE(4) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4156]` ` &       'UNIT 63 FORMAT WILL BE COMPACT ASCII.') `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4228]` ` CASE(4) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4386]` ` CASE(4) `) 관측소 NOUTE·NOUTV·NOUTM·NOUTC는 절댓값 4를 거부한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3647]` ` CASE(4,6:) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3648]` ` write(scratchMessage, '(A,I0,A)') 'NOUTE value of ', NOUTE, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3649]` ` &                           " is invalid for elevation recording station output." `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3650]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message=scratchMessage) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4154]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4155]` ` CALL logMessage(INFO, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4156]` ` &       'UNIT 63 FORMAT WILL BE COMPACT ASCII.') `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4228]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4386]` ` CASE(4) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3647]` ` CASE(4,6:) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3650]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message=scratchMessage) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4154]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4156]` ` &       'UNIT 63 FORMAT WILL BE COMPACT ASCII.') `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4228]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4230]` ` &       'UNIT 64 FORMAT WILL BE COMPACT ASCII.') `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4386]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4388]` ` &          'UNIT 73 AND 74 FORMATS WILL BE COMPACT ASCII.') `)
  - `7` = XDMF (global only)
- Set in `read_input.F` at `[file=src/read_input.F line=3613-3633, 4124-4152]`
- Writer dispatch by specifier at `[file=src/write_output.F line=4226-4250]` (`ASCII/SPARSE_ASCII/NETCDF3/NETCDF4/XDMF`)

### ASCII headers

- `header61` at `[file=src/globalio.F line=963-977]`
- `header63` at `[file=src/globalio.F line=1011-1024]`
- 2차원 ASCII 시계열의 첫 줄은 RUNDES,RUNID,AGRID이다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=971]` ` write(lun, 1000) rundes, runid, agrid `)
- 숫자 줄은 자료 세트 수·공간 수·DTDP*NSPOOL·NSPOOL·자료 성분 수를 쓴다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=972]` ` write(lun, 1010) ntrspe, size_this, dtdp*nspoole, nspoole, 1, `; `[file=src/globalio.F line=1020]` ` write(lun, 1010) ndsetse, size_this, dtdp*nspoolge, nspoolge, 1, `)
- native writer는 FileFmtVersion 표지를 덧붙인다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=973]` ` &                 FileFmtVersion `; `[file=src/globalio.F line=1021]` ` &                 FileFmtVersion `)
- 3차원 헤더와 핫스타트 헤더는 아래 E절과 출력 차이 표에서 구분한다.

### NetCDF naming

- Base file: `.../fort.61` or `.../fort.63` at `[file=src/write_output.F line=1568-1575, 1691-1693]`
- NetCDF check uses appended `.nc` (so `fort.63.nc` is the actual file on disk)

## B. fort.62/64 (velocity)

- Mirror of elevation structure at `[file=src/write_output.F line=537-563, 710-714]`
- fort.62: `NOUTV/NSPOOLV`, fort.64: `NOUTGV/NSPOOLGV`
- `num_items_per_record = 2` (U,V components) at `[file=src/write_output.F line=548-557, 716-720]`
- Headers `header62` at `[file=src/globalio.F line=987-1001]`, `header64` at `[file=src/globalio.F line=1059-1073]` — encode type=2 for 2-component output

## C. fort.71-74 (met output)

- Station pressure/wind (71/72) controlled by `NOUTM` at `[file=src/write_output.F line=735-748, 775-792]`
- Global pressure/wind (73/74) controlled by `NOUTGW` at `[file=src/write_output.F line=817-823, 906-913]`
- 기상 관측소는 절댓값 1·3·5를 허용한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4023]` ` CASE(3) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4029]` ` CASE(5) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4035]` ` CASE(4,6:) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4036]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message="This NOUTM value is invalid") `)
- 기상 전역 출력은 절댓값 1·3·4·5·7을 허용한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4374]` ` CASE(1) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4380]` ` CASE(3) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4386]` ` CASE(4) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4389]` ` CASE(5) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4395]` ` CASE(7) `)
- 절댓값 2는 종료한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4377]` ` CASE(2) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4378]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4379]` ` &                     message='Binary format output is no longer supported.') `)
- **Disabled when NWS=0** at `[file=src/write_output.F line=760-762, 804-806, 828-830, 920-922]`
- Pressure and wind kept in separate descriptors/arrays (not combined)

- Init: `initHarmonicParameters` called from `[file=src/adcirc.F line=268]`
- TIMESTEP은 단계마다 updateHarmonicAnalysis(IT, TIMEH)를 호출한다. (`[file=src/timestep.F line=1188]` ` CALL updateHarmonicAnalysis(IT, TIMEH) `)
- Accumulator: `harm.F:632-689` accumulates per-frequency least-squares terms over requested window
- Sub-routines `LSQUPD*` for station/global elevation and velocity
- **End-of-run solve + write**: `solveHarmonicAnalysis` then `writeHarmonicAnalysisOutput` at `[file=src/adcirc.F line=540-541]`
- Writer: `writeOutHarmonicArrays` at `[file=src/write_output.F line=2673]`
  - ASCII output at `[file=src/write_output.F line=2701-2750]`
  - NetCDF output at `[file=src/write_output.F line=2752-2760]`
- `NFREQ` usage: `[file=src/read_input.F line=4421-4452, 4500]`, `[file=src/harm.F line=128, 547-549]`
- **`MUFREQ` not present** — use `NFREQ` only

| D19 / fort.43·46 / EV 의미 | 매개변수 문서는 horizontal eddy viscosity로 설명한다. 코드는 수직 난류 출력 EV를 eddy viscosity로 기록한다. fort.46 문서는 vertical을 적는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2754]` ` Spatially constant horizontal eddy viscosity for the momentum equations (units of length2/time) for station output `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2784]` ` Horizontal eddy viscosity for the momentum equations (units of length2/time) for global output `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3749]` ` EVSta(NN,k) =EV(N1,k) *StaI3DT1(NN) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=885]` ` 'station water column vertically varying eddy viscosity' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=891]` ` 'station_water_eddy_viscosity_vertically_varying' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=900]` ` call putUnitsAttribute(sta%ncid, sta%w_station_data_id, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=901]` ` "m^2/s", "n/a") ` |
| D20 / fort.47 / 생성 조건·출력 간격 | 문서는 NSPOOLGE를 적는다. 코드는 I3DGD와 NSPO3DGD를 사용한다. IDEN은 양수 3 또는 4이어야 한다. BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0 조건도 필요하다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=6]` `` The fort.47 file records the top temperature boundary condition that is either provided via output from an atmospheric model or as an input variable into the code via a Surface Temperature Boundary Values File. This output file follows the same format as the :doc:`Water Surface Elevation <fort63>` file, as it only records the temperature values for the surface layer. The output file is only provided if the :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` value in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <../input_files/fort15>` is given as a 3 or 4, as ADCIRC evaluates the temperature changes for these two :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` values. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=17]` ` * Output timing matches fort.63 parameters (NSPOOLGE) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1053]` ` IF (ABS(I3DGD).EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1055]` ` IF((IDEN.EQ.3).OR.(IDEN.EQ.4)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1056]` ` IF (BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1059]` ` WRITE(47,498) NDSet3DGD,NP,DTDP*NSpo3DGD,NSpo3DGD,1 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3880]` ` IF((IDEN.EQ.3).OR.(IDEN.EQ.4)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3881]` ` IF(BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0) THEN `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3882]` ` OPEN(47,FILE=TRIM(LOCALDIR)//'/'//'fort.47', ` |
| D20 / fort.47 / 헤더 DTDP*NSPO3DGD | 코드는 fort.63 계열의 다섯 숫자 형태를 쓰지만 시간 제어는 3차원 밀도 출력이다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=6]` `` The fort.47 file records the top temperature boundary condition that is either provided via output from an atmospheric model or as an input variable into the code via a Surface Temperature Boundary Values File. This output file follows the same format as the :doc:`Water Surface Elevation <fort63>` file, as it only records the temperature values for the surface layer. The output file is only provided if the :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` value in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <../input_files/fort15>` is given as a 3 or 4, as ADCIRC evaluates the temperature changes for these two :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` values. ``<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1058]` ` WRITE(47,499) RUNDES,RUNID,AGRID `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1059]` ` WRITE(47,498) NDSet3DGD,NP,DTDP*NSpo3DGD,NSpo3DGD,1 ` |

This output file follows the same format as the :doc:`Water Surface Elevation <fort63>` file, as it only records the temperature values for the surface layer. The output file is only provided if the :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` value in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <../input_files/fort15>` is given as a 3 or 4, as ADCIRC evaluates the temperature changes for these two :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` values. ``<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1058]` ` WRITE(47,499) RUNDES,RUNID,AGRID `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1059]` ` WRITE(47,498) NDSet3DGD,NP,DTDP*NSpo3DGD,NSpo3DGD,1 ` |
| D20 / POST47 / 헤더·TIME/IT·수온 | 합치기는 NSPO3DGD를 쓴다. 문서는 NSPOOLGE를 적는다. 수온 자료는 전역 노드 번호로 다시 쓴다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=6]` `` The fort.47 file records the top temperature boundary condition that is either provided via output from an atmospheric model or as an input variable into the code via a Surface Temperature Boundary Values File. This output file follows the same format as the :doc:`Water Surface Elevation <fort63>` file, as it only records the temperature values for the surface layer. The output file is only provided if the :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` value in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <../input_files/fort15>` is given as a 3 or 4, as ADCIRC evaluates the temperature changes for these two :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` values. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=17]` ` * Output timing matches fort.63 parameters (NSPOOLGE) `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5241]` ` WRITE(47,'(A85)') INLINE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5242]` ` WRITE(47,3645) NDSET,NNODG,DT,NSPO3DGD,IRTYPE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5248]` ` IF (IPROC.EQ.1) WRITE(47,2110) TIMEOUTE,ITE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5258]` ` CALL NEWINDEX(TOPTEMP(I),OUTMSG,I) `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5259]` ` WRITE(47,*) TRIM(OUTMSG) !jgf46.00 TRIM to cut down file size ` |
| D21 / fort.45·46 / IRTYPE=3 의미 | cold start는 밀도 헤더 준비 후의 IRType를 재사용한다. IDEN=0·1이면 1이다. IDEN=2·3이면 2이다. 문서의 3D velocity record type 3과 다르다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2250]` ` The record type (= 1 for elevation files, = 2 for velocity files, and = 3 for 3D velocity file) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1041]` ` IF((IDen.EQ.1).OR.(IDen.EQ.0)) IRType=1 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1042]` ` IF((IDen.EQ.2).OR.(IDen.EQ.3)) IRType=2 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1043]` ` IF(IDen.EQ.4) IRType=3 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1081]` ` CALL initOutput3D(45, I3DGV, NDSET3DGV, NP, NP_G, `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1082]` ` &                  NSPO3DGV, IRType, I3DGVRec) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1085]` ` CALL initOutput3D(46, I3DGT, NDSET3DGT, NP, NP_G, `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1086]` ` &                  NSPO3DGT, IRType, I3DGTRec) ` |
| D44 / fort.42.nc·fort.45.nc / Cartesian 수직 유속 방향 | 문서는 WZSTA를 수직 유속으로 정의한다. Cartesian w-vel3D의 positive 속성은 x 유속에서 반시계 90도라고 적는다. 해석: 이 문장은 수직 방향을 나타내지 않는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2734]` ` Vertical velocity for station output. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, velocity is m/s, if gravity is in ft/s2, velocity is in ft/s). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the velocity units are m/s. `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=830]` ` att_text = 'station_z_water_velocity_vertically_varying' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=835]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(sta%ncid, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=836]` ` sta%w_station_data_id, 'positive', & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=837]` ` '90 degrees counterclockwise from x water velocity') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1685]` ` att_text = 'station_z_water_velocity_vertically_varying' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1690]` ` dat%w_nodal_data_id, 'positive', & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1691]` ` '90 degrees counterclockwise from x water velocity') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1692]` ` call check_err(iret) ` |
| D66 / fort.41.nc·fort.44.nc / G의 otherwise 단위 속성 | 문서는 SIGTSTA를 kg/m^3로 적는다. SALSTA를 PSU로 적는다. TEMPSTA를 DEG C로 적는다. G<11.d0이면 해당 속성이 맞는다. otherwise 분기는 관측소 SIGT·SAL에 n/a를 쓴다. 관측소·전역 TEMP에 Fahrenheit를 쓴다. 전역 SIGT에도 n/a를 쓴다. 전역 SAL의 PSU는 같다. 이는 출력 단위 속성의 차이이다. 실제 온도 값의 변환 정확도는 실행하지 않았다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2704]` ` Sigma T value (kg/m^3) (=density-1000) at a specified recording station `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2709]` ` Salinity (PSU) at a specified recording station `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2714]` ` Temperature (DEG C) at a specified recording station `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=700]` ` "kg/m^3", "n/a") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=715]` ` "PSU", "n/a") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=732]` ` "Celsius", "Fahrenheit") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1570]` ` "kg/m^3", "n/a") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1582]` ` "PSU", "PSU") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1595]` ` "Celsius", "Fahrenheit") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10455]` ` if (G < 11.d0) then `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10456]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(ncid, var_id, 'units', metric) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10457]` ` else `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10458]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(ncid, var_id, 'units', english) ` |


- Controls: `I3DSD` for fort.41 (density/salinity/temp), `I3DSV` for fort.42 (velocity) at `[file=src/global_3dvs.F line=229-264]`
- Init: `initOutput3D` writes 3D headers at `[file=src/write_output.F line=2787-2843]`
- Descriptor setup: `[file=src/write_output.F line=2980-3103]`
- NFEN은 descriptor 한 배열의 층 수이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=3043]` ` SigTStaDescript % num_items_per_record =  NFEN `)
- fort.42 한 관측소 자료 줄은 층마다 U,V,W 세 값을 쓴다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=3687]` ` WRITE(42,1104) NN, `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=3688]` ` &                  (REAL(qSta(NN,k)),AIMAG(qSta(NN,k)), `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=3689]` ` &                  WZSta(NN,k),k=1,NFEN) `)
- fort.41의 층별 값 수는 ABS(IDEN)에 따라 1·2·3이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=3043]` ` SigTStaDescript % num_items_per_record =  NFEN `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3517]` ` WRITE(41,1100) TimeLoc,IT,(Sigma(k),k=1,NFEN-1) `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3524]` ` WRITE(41,1100) TimeLoc,IT,(Sigma(k),Sigma(k), `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3528]` ` WRITE(41,1104) NN, `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3546]` ` WRITE(41,1104) NN,(SigTSta(NN,k), `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3547]` ` &                      SalSta(NN,k),TempSta(NN,k),k=1,NFEN) `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3687]` ` WRITE(42,1104) NN, `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3688]` ` &                  (REAL(qSta(NN,k)),AIMAG(qSta(NN,k)), `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3689]` ` &                  WZSta(NN,k),k=1,NFEN) `)
- SIGMA 반복 수와 마지막 좌표는 아래 출력 차이 표의 D17로 구분한다.
- Layerwise loop `k=1,NFEN` at `[file=src/write_output.F line=3403-3416, 3584-3591, 3623-3630]`

- TIMESTEP은 단계마다 collectMinMaxData(timeloc)를 호출한다. (`[file=src/timestep.F line=1166]` ` call collectMinMaxData(timeloc) `)
- collectMinMaxData의 정의는 src/write_output.F:6432에 있다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=6432]` ` subroutine collectMinMaxData(timeloc) `)
- Descriptor init at `[file=src/write_output.F line=1205-1237, 1239-1268]` (both start with `writeFlag=.false.`, `readMaxMin=.true.` for hotstart continuity)

침수 시간·최대 침수 깊이·건조 이력도 이전 파일을 읽는 경로를 갖는다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1462]` ` InundationTimeDescript % readMaxMin           = inundationOutput `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1497]` ` MaxInunDepthDescript % readMaxMin           = inundationOutput `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1565]` ` EverDriedDescript % readMaxMin           = inundationOutput `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1699]` ` if (ihot.ne.0) then `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1702]` ` if ( ptr(i) % descript2D % readMaxMin.eqv..true.) then `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1706]` ` call readAndMapToSubdomainMaxMin(ptr(i) % descript2D, `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1731]` ` call readAndMapToSubdomainMaxMinNetCDF( `)

|---|---|---|
| D31 / maxinundepth·inundationtime·everdried / hot start 누적 범위 | 문서는 현재 실행만 반영한다고 제한한다. 코드는 기존 파일을 찾으면 이전 배열을 읽어 이어 갱신한다. 미래 시각 거부 조건은 max/min 공통 행과 같다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/maxinundepth63.rst line=36]` ` * The values only reflect the current run, even if the run was hotstarted `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/inundationtime63.rst line=36]` ` * The values only reflect the current run, even if the run was hotstarted `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/everdried63.rst line=38]` ` * The values only reflect the current run, even if the run was hotstarted `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1462]` ` InundationTimeDescript % readMaxMin           = inundationOutput `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1497]` ` MaxInunDepthDescript % readMaxMin           = inundationOutput `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1565]` ` EverDriedDescript % readMaxMin           = inundationOutput `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1699]` ` if (ihot.ne.0) then `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1702]` ` if ( ptr(i) % descript2D % readMaxMin.eqv..true.) then `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1706]` ` call readAndMapToSubdomainMaxMin(ptr(i) % descript2D, `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=1731]` ` call readAndMapToSubdomainMaxMinNetCDF( ` |

- writeOutput2D는 `if (it.eq.nt) then` 조건에서 극값 파일을 쓴다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1949]` ` if (it.eq.nt) then `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1951]` ` CALL writeOutArrayMinMax(311, TimeLoc, IT, 2, EtaMaxDescript, `)

극값 파일 쓰기는 위의 it.eq.nt 조건을 따른다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1949]` ` if (it.eq.nt) then `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1951]` ` CALL writeOutArrayMinMax(311, TimeLoc, IT, 2, EtaMaxDescript, `)

## G. NetCDF vs ASCII

| 유형·파일·변수 | 문서·코드 차이 | 출처와 원문 |
|---|---|---|
| D42 / netCDF 출력의 격자 포함 설명 | 관측소 출력은 station 좌표와 변수만 정의한다. 전역 mesh의 element 연결과 경계 변수를 정의하지 않는다. 해석: 문서의 일반 설명을 관측소 netCDF까지 적용하면 맞지 않는다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/visualization.rst line=41]` ` -  NetCDF ADCIRC output files self-contain mesh node, connectivity, and `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/visualization.rst line=42]` ` boundary information. `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=629]` ` iret = nf90_def_dim(sta%ncid, 'station', & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=650]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(sta%ncid, 'station_name', NF90_CHAR, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=656]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(sta%ncid, 'x', NF90_DOUBLE, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=660]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(sta%ncid, 'y', NF90_DOUBLE, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=666]` ` call defineCoordinateAttributes(sta%ncid, sta%x_id, sta%y_id) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1534]` ` call defineMeshVariables(dat%ncid, dat%myMesh, dat%myFile) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=9494]` ` iret = nf90_def_dim(ncid, 'node', myMesh%num_nodes, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=9553]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(ncid, 'element', NF90_INT, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=9575]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(ncid, 'nvdll', NF90_INT, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=9585]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(ncid, 'nbdv', NF90_INT, & ` |
| D47 / fort.77.nc / 시간 차원 | 문서는 시간마다 노드 자료를 기록한다. weir_dz 변수는 node 차원 하나로 정의한다. 쓰기 루틴은 시계열 start/count를 전달한다. 해석: 변수 스키마에는 시간별 값을 구분할 차원이 없다. 라이브러리 오류 반환은 실행하지 않았다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort77.rst line=4]` `` This file contains time-varying weir elevation data when time-varying weirs are activated and output is requested in ADCIRC. The file records changes in elevation from the original elevations specified in the :doc:`Grid and Boundary Information File <../input_files/fort14>`. When written in sparse format, the data is recorded only for weir nodes, with node numbers referenced to the global node numbering system. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort77.rst line=17]` `` :ref:`TIME <TIME>`, :ref:`IT <IT>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort77.rst line=20]` `` k, :ref:`TVW(k) <TVW>` ``<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2677]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'weir_dz' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2678]` ` dat%nodal_data_dims_max(1) = dat%myMesh%num_nodes_dim_id `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2679]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(dat%ncid, trim(dat%varnames(1)), & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2680]` ` NF90_DOUBLE, dat%nodal_data_dims_max, dat%nodal_data_id) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2695]` ` 'coordinates', 'y x') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=4640]` ` kount(1) = dat%myMesh%num_nodes `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=4641]` ` kount(2) = dat%myTime%timenc_len `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=4642]` ` start(1) = 1 `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=4643]` ` start(2) = dat%myFile%record_counter `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=5064]` ` case (47, 63, 73, 77, 90, 93, 173, 301, 302, 303, 304, 306, 307, 108) ! | D60 / depth / English 길이 단위 | 코드는 DP 배열을 그대로 저장한다. units는 m으로 고정한다. 해석: 문서가 허용하는 English 길이 단위의 Cartesian 실행에서는 값의 단위와 속성이 다르다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=58]` ` Bathymetric depth with respect to the geoid, positive below the geoid and negative above the geoid. G 문서는 중력의 단위가 모델의 길이 단위를 정한다고 적는다. writer는 경계 높이와 관 직경을 변환 없이 복사한다. units는 m으로 고정한다. 해석: English 길이 단위의 Cartesian 실행에서는 barht·pipediam의 값과 속성이 다르다. The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at these nodes, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j) and <b >BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(IBCONN(k,j)). If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=133]` ` Cross barrier pipe diameter in internal barrier with cross barrier pipes (diameter in units consistent with the mesh). `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=492]` ` Gravitational constant. - Specifier-driven at `[file=src/write_output.F line=4226-4271]`:
  - ASCII / sparse ASCII → text writer
  - NETCDF3 / NETCDF4 → NetCDF writer
- NetCDF init only for NETCDF3/NETCDF4 at `[file=src/write_output.F line=1611-1615]`
- Compile-time `ADCNETCDF` guard required
- File naming: base `file_name` no extension at `[file=src/write_output.F line=1572-1580]`; NetCDF treated as `file_name + '.nc'` at `[file=src/write_output.F line=1691-1693]`

## G2. XDMF 출력 + 보조 모듈 (전소스 커버, 2026-06-04)

- native XDMF 출력 파일은 src/xdmfio.F이다. (`[file=src/xdmfio.F line=52]` ` if (abs(descript%specifier).ne.XDMF) then `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=78]` ` call xdmfInit(descript % xdmfMD % xdmfFortranObj) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=369]` ` call XdmfWrite(descript % xdmfMD % xdmfFortranObj, `)
- writer는 .h5와 .xmf 파일을 짝으로 쓴다. (`[file=src/xdmfio.F line=81]` ` &    trim(descript % file_name) //'.h5'//char(0), release) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=370]` ` &   trim(descript % file_name)//'.xmf'//char(0), `)
- 변수 메타데이터 호출은 이 경로에서 주석이다. (`[file=src/xdmfio.F line=187]` ` !descript % xdmfmd % variable_name = 'depth' `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=188]` ` !descript % xdmfmd % long_name = 'distance from geoid' `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=193]` ` !call writeMetaData(xdmfFortranObj, md) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=258]` ` ! set the metadata for this dataset `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=259]` ` !call writeMetaData(xdmfFortranObj, md) `)

이 native writer의 지원 논리 단위는 63·64·73·74·93이다. (`[file=src/xdmfio.F line=217]` ` select case(descript % lun) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=218]` ` case(63) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=224]` ` case(64) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=231]` ` case(73) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=237]` ` case(74) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=244]` ` case(93) `)
그 밖의 논리 단위는 terminate를 호출한다. (`[file=src/xdmfio.F line=250]` ` case default `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=251]` ` write(scratchMessage,'("Cannot write unit ",i0," files.")') descript % lun `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=252]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message=scratchMessage) `)
XDMF/HDF5 라이브러리 내부 형식과 실행 결과는 확인하지 않음.

- **`netcdf_error.F90`**: NetCDF 호출 에러 핸들링(nf90 상태 체크). `xdmfio`/NetCDF writer 보조.
- **`hashtable.F90`**: 해시테이블 자료구조(노드/요소 lookup 가속). `subgridLookup.F90`: subgrid correction lookup(부분침수 보정). `gl2loc_mapping.F90`: global↔local 노드 매핑([[adcirc-parallel-implementation]] MPI). `terminate.F90`: 정상/이상 종료. `vew1d.F90`: vertical element wall 1D channel([[adcirc-weir-boundary]] IBTYPE 64 관련).

## H. Post-merge (parallel)

| 유형·파일·변수 | 문서·코드 차이 | 출처와 원문 |
|---|---|---|
| D61 / SWAN 출력 개수 제한 | 문서 앞부분은 TM01·TM02·WIND의 가능성도 언급한다. 문서의 Method 1 설명은 기존 파일 네 개를 한도로 적는다. v56.2.1은 HS, DIR, TM01, TPS, WIND, TM02, TMM10의 일곱 descriptor를 갖는다. 최대값 출력도 각각 연결한다. SWAN 라이브러리 내부는 읽지 않았다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/coupled_adcirc_swan.rst line=77]` ` -  and perhaps others like TM01, TM02, and WIND `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/coupled_adcirc_swan.rst line=163]` ` existing SWAN output that you'd like to modify. Keep in mind that you only have `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/coupled_adcirc_swan.rst line=164]` ` four existing files to work with here, so that is ultimately your limit on `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/coupled_adcirc_swan.rst line=168]` ` consider renaming swan_DIR.63 to swan_PDIR.63. If you need more than four output `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/tips_and_tricks/coupled_adcirc_swan.rst line=169]` ` parameters and also want to change the filenames, see Method 2 below. `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=5884]` ` SwanHSDescript % file_basename        = "swan_HS" `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=5930]` ` SwanDIRDescript % file_extension       = 63 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=5976]` ` SwanTM01Descript % file_extension       = 63 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6022]` ` SwanTPSDescript % file_extension       = 63 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6070]` ` SwanWindDescript % file_extension       = 64 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6116]` ` SwanTM02Descript % file_extension       = 63 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6162]` ` SwanTMM10Descript % file_extension       = 63 ` |
| D64 / windDrag.173 / 이름·조건·간격 | 문서는 winddrag.173을 적는다. 코드는 windDrag를 basename으로 쓴다. 문서는 fort.63 또는 fort.73/74 설정을 허용한다. 이 descriptor는 NOUTGW와 전역 기상 출력 창·간격만 연결한다. 활성 조건 outputWindDrag는 같다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=142]` ` In all cases, the actual wind drag coefficients determined by ADCIRC can be `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=143]` `` output to a :ref:`fort.63 <fort63>`-type file named ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=144]` ``` ``winddrag.173``. Output settings (file format, output start/end ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=145]` ` times, and output interval) match those of either the fort.63 or fort.73/74 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=146]` ` files. Outputting of this file is enabled by setting `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=147]` ``` ``outputWindDrag=.TRUE.`` in the ``&metControl`` namelist of the :ref:`fort.15 file's ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=148]` `` namelist section <fort15>`. ``<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=955]` ` windDragDescript % lun                  = 173 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=956]` ` windDragDescript % specifier            = NOUTGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=957]` ` windDragDescript % startTimeStep           = NTCYSGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=958]` ` windDragDescript % endTimeStep             = NTCYFGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=959]` ` windDragDescript % outputTimeStepIncrement = NSPOOLGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=963]` ` windDragDescript % field_name           = 'windDrag' `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=964]` ` windDragDescript % file_basename        = 'windDrag' `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=966]` ` windDragDescript % writeFlag            = outputWindDrag ` |
| D65 / POST71 / 헤더 NSTAM | 헤더는 마지막 지역 파일에서 읽은 NUMSTNS를 쓴다. 자료 루프는 전역 NSTAM을 사용한다. 해석: 마지막 지역 관측소 수와 전역 관측소 수가 다르면 헤더와 자료 수가 다르다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort71.rst line=16]` `` :ref:`NTRSPM <NTRSPM>`, :ref:`NSTAM <NSTAM>`, :ref:`DTDP <DTDP>`\*:ref:`NSPOOLM <NSPOOLM>`, :ref:`NSPOOLM <NSPOOLM>`, :ref:`IRTYPE <IRTYPE>` ``<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1913]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),3645) NTRSPM,NUMSTNS,DTM,NSTEMP,ITEMPM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1914]` ` IF (NUMSTNS.NE.NSTAMP(IPROC)) THEN `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1924]` ` WRITE(71,3645) NTRSPM,NUMSTNS,DTM,NSPOOLM,ITEMPM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1937]` ` WRITE(71,2120) TIMEOUTSM,ITSM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1938]` ` DO I=1, NSTAM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1940]` ` WRITE(71,*) TRIM(OUTMSG) !jgf46.00 TRIM to cut down file size ` |
| D65 / POST91 / 헤더 NSTAM | 헤더는 마지막 지역 파일에서 읽은 NUMSTNS를 쓴다. 자료 루프는 전역 NSTAM을 사용한다. 해석: 마지막 지역 관측소 수와 전역 관측소 수가 다르면 헤더와 자료 수가 다르다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort91.rst line=14]` `` :ref:`NTRSPM <NTRSPM>`, :ref:`NSTAM <NSTAM>`, :ref:`DTDP <DTDP>` * :ref:`NSPOOLM <NSPOOLM>`, :ref:`NSPOOLM <NSPOOLM>`, :ref:`IRTYPE <IRTYPE>` ``<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=2982]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),3645) NTRSPM,NUMSTNS,DTM,NSTEMP,ITEMPM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=2983]` ` IF (NUMSTNS.NE.NSTAMP(IPROC)) THEN `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=2993]` ` WRITE(91,3645) NTRSPM,NUMSTNS,DTM,NSPOOLM,ITEMPM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=3006]` ` WRITE(91,2120) TIMEOUTSM,ITSM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=3007]` ` DO I=1, NSTAM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=3009]` ` WRITE(91,*) TRIM(OUTMSG) !jgf46.00 TRIM to cut down file size ` |
| D65 / POST43 / 헤더 관측소 수 | 합치기는 마지막 지역 헤더의 NSTA를 출력 헤더에 쓴다. 자료 루프는 NSTA3DT를 사용한다. 해석: 마지막 지역 수와 전역 수가 다르면 헤더와 자료 수가 다르다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort43.rst line=15]` `` :ref:`NDSET3DST <NDSET3DST>`, :ref:`NSTA3DT <NSTA3DT>`, :ref:`DTDP <DTDP>` * :ref:`NSPO3DST <NSPO3DST>`, :ref:`NSPO3DST <NSPO3DST>`, :ref:`NFEN <NFEN>`, :ref:`IRTYPE <IRTYPE>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort43.rst line=20]` `` for j=1, :ref:`NSTA3DT <NSTA3DT>` ``<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4594]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),3645) NDSET,NSTA,DT,NSPO3DST,NFEN,IRTYPE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4610]` ` WRITE(43,3645) NDSET,NSTA,DT,NSPO3DST,NFEN,IRTYPE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4644]` ` DO I=1,NSTA3DT `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4645]` ` WRITE(43,1104) I,((FDVar(I,M,L),M=1,NVar),L=1,NFEN) ` |


### XDMF Information과 경계 자료 (v56.2.1)

조건이 있는 Information은 writeControlXDMF의 조건에서만 기록한다. 숫자 포맷은 문자열 저장 폭이다. 이 숫자는 물리 단위 변환 결과가 아니다. ([출력 대조 보고서 §2.8](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-outputs-vs-code.md#28-xdmf-출력))

|---|---|---|

| 관로 경계의 BARINHT·BARINCFSB·BARINCFSP | 관로 경계도 장벽 높이와 두 유량 계수 Attribute를 기록한다. 단위 변환은 확인하지 않음. | `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=545]` ` case(5,25) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=554]` ` &          'BARINHT'//CHAR(0), `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=555]` ` &          XDMF_ATTRIBUTE_CENTER_NODE, XDMF_ATTRIBUTE_TYPE_SCALAR, nvell(i), `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=556]` ` &          XDMF_ARRAY_TYPE_FLOAT64, internalFluxBoundariesWithPipes(ifwpCount)%barinht) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=559]` ` &          'BARINCFSB'//CHAR(0), `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=560]` ` &          XDMF_ATTRIBUTE_CENTER_NODE, XDMF_ATTRIBUTE_TYPE_SCALAR, nvell(i), `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=561]` ` &          XDMF_ARRAY_TYPE_FLOAT64, internalFluxBoundariesWithPipes(ifwpCount)%barincfsb) `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=564]` ` &          'BARINCFSP'//CHAR(0), `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=565]` ` &          XDMF_ATTRIBUTE_CENTER_NODE, XDMF_ATTRIBUTE_TYPE_SCALAR, nvell(i), `; `[file=src/xdmfio.F line=566]` ` &          XDMF_ARRAY_TYPE_FLOAT64, internalFluxBoundariesWithPipes(ifwpCount)%barincfsp) ` |


- Per-PE files: `PE0000/fort.6X` etc.
- Master mapping from `fort.80` at `[file=prep/post.F line=67-164]` includes:
  - Global/local node maps
  - Station maps
  - Output control / stride fields
- Merge routines per output type:
  - `POST61` at `[file=prep/post.F line=238]`
  - `POST62` at `[file=prep/post.F line=492]`
  - `POST63` at `[file=prep/post.F line=750]`
  - `POST64` at `[file=prep/post.F line=1003]`
  - `POST71` at `[file=prep/post.F line=1858]`
  - `POST72` at `[file=prep/post.F line=2125]`
  - `POST73` at `[file=prep/post.F line=2404]`
  - `POST74` at `[file=prep/post.F line=2660]`

병렬 합치기는 전역 파일을 쓴다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1938]` ` DO I=1, NSTAM `; `[file=prep/post.F line=3007]` ` DO I=1, NSTAM `; `[file=prep/post.F line=4644]` ` DO I=1,NSTA3DT `)
POST71·POST91·POST43의 헤더 공간 수 차이는 아래 출력 차이 표의 D65로 구분한다.
POST41의 마지막 SIGMA 두 원소는 아래 출력 차이 표의 D17로 구분한다.
일반 ASCII 합치기·전용 writer·netCDF·핫스타트 합치기의 지원 범위는 별도로 읽는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1924]` ` WRITE(71,3645) NTRSPM,NUMSTNS,DTM,NSPOOLM,ITEMPM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=1938]` ` DO I=1, NSTAM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=2993]` ` WRITE(91,3645) NTRSPM,NUMSTNS,DTM,NSPOOLM,ITEMPM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=3007]` ` DO I=1, NSTAM `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4610]` ` WRITE(43,3645) NDSET,NSTA,DT,NSPO3DST,NFEN,IRTYPE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4644]` ` DO I=1,NSTA3DT `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4336]` ` WRITE(41,1100) TIMEOUT,IT, `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4337]` ` &        ((SIGMA(M,N),M=1,NVar),N=1,NFEN-1), `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=4338]` ` &        SIGMA(1,NFEN),SIGMA(2,NFEN) `)

## Decision Guide — output stride

| Use case | NSPOOLE / NSPOOLGE | Output frequency |
|----------|--------------------|------------------|
| Tidal validation | NSPOOLE = 900 (DT=2s → 30min) | per 30 min |
| Storm surge prediction | NSPOOLE = 360 (15 min) | finer for time-critical |
| Climatology | NSPOOLE = 86400 (1 day) | daily averaging needed externally |
| Disk-saving runs | NSPOOLE = 1800 (1 hour) | rough validation only |

예시 산정 (RNDAY=30, DT=2s, NSPOOLGE=900, 475K 노드):
- 30 days × 24 h × 2 (per hour) = ~1440 records on global elevation field
- Each record = 475K × 8 bytes = 3.8 MB → total ~5.5 GB fort.63.nc

## Working Rules

1. useNetCDF가 TRUE이고 NETCDF_AVAIL이 FALSE이면 코드는 종료한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4756]` ` IF ((useNetCDF.eqv..true.).and.(NETCDF_AVAIL.eqv..false.)) THEN `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4757]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message="NetCDF input and/or output was" `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4758]` ` &     //" indicated in the control parameters of the fort.15 file" `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4759]` ` &     //" but it is not supported by this ADCIRC executable" `) ADCNETCDF가 없는 일반 writer는 netCDF 요청에서 terminate를 호출한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=4341]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=4342]` ` &         message='NetCDF was specified but this executable was '// `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=4343]` ` &                 'not compiled with NetCDF support.') `) 전용 writer의 미지원 경로는 오류 메시지를 쓴다. (`[file=src/writer.F line=715]` ` write(scratchMessage,'(a)') `; `[file=src/writer.F line=716]` ` &          'NetCDF is not available. No output has been written.' `; `[file=src/writer.F line=717]` ` call allMessage(ERROR, scratchMessage) `)
2. 핫스타트 후 음수 NOUTE/NOUTGE는 새 파일을 시작한다. (`[file=src/hstart.F line=1562]` ` IF(NOUTE.LT.0) THEN `; `[file=src/hstart.F line=1573]` ` IF(NOUTE.EQ.-1) THEN `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3888]` ` if ((IHOT == 0) .or. (descript%specifier < 0) .or. & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3889]` ` (myFile%fileFound .eqv. .false.)) then `) 핫스타트 후 양수 제어값은 기존 파일에 이어 쓴다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=858]` ` NOUTE = 1 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in standard ascii format. Following a hot start, continued output is merged into the existing fort.61 file. `) netCDF는 cold start 또는 파일 부재에서도 새 파일을 만든다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3888]` ` if ((IHOT == 0) .or. (descript%specifier < 0) .or. & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3889]` ` (myFile%fileFound .eqv. .false.)) then `) 기존 netCDF는 시각에 따라 기록 위치를 정한다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=5188]` ` if ((t%timenc(1) < storedTimes(i)) .or. & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=5189]` ` (abs(t%timenc(1) - storedTimes(i)) < 1.0d-10)) then `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=5228]` ` iret = nf90_put_var(ncid, t%timenc_id, t%timenc, starti, counti) `)

NOUTE<0 분기는 NSCOUE를 0으로 대입한다. (`[file=src/hstart.F line=1562]` ` IF(NOUTE.LT.0) THEN `; `[file=src/hstart.F line=1563]` ` NSCOUE=0 `)

3. writeOutput2D는 `if (it.eq.nt) then` 조건에서 극값 파일을 쓴다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1949]` ` if (it.eq.nt) then `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1951]` ` CALL writeOutArrayMinMax(311, TimeLoc, IT, 2, EtaMaxDescript, `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1955]` ` CALL writeOutArrayMinMax(312, TimeLoc, IT, 2, UMaxDescript, `) ADCIRC_Run의 earlyterminate 경로는 ITIME_END를 IT 인수로 넘겨 writeOutput2D를 호출한다. (`[file=src/adcirc.F line=485]` ` if ( earlyterminate.ne.0 ) then `; `[file=src/adcirc.F line=486]` ` call writeOutput2D(ITIME_END,TimeLoc) `; `[file=src/adcirc.F line=487]` ` exit `) ITIME_END.EQ.NT이면 이 경로도 극값 파일을 쓸 수 있다. (`[file=src/adcirc.F line=486]` ` call writeOutput2D(ITIME_END,TimeLoc) `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1949]` ` if (it.eq.nt) then `) 외부 kill이나 비정상 프로세스 종료의 파일 결과는 확인하지 않음.
4. **Harmonic output is end-of-run** — accumulators are per-step but solve+write happens once.
5. **Per-PE outputs need post-merge** via `adcpost` (separate executable) or `POST61` etc. routines invoked manually.

## Common Pitfalls

- **fort.63 + fort.63.nc both exist** — old format vs new NetCDF; `read_global` may pick the older. Delete one or set NOUT specifier consistently.
- **Hot restart with different `NSPOOLE`** — old file format matched first stride; new stride creates inconsistent records.
- **`NFREQ` mismatch between fort.15 NBFR and fort.15 harmonic block** — silent corruption.
- **Adcprep `--prepall` re-runs every time** — overrides per-PE PE####/fort.6X files. Check before re-running mid-output collection.
- v56.2.1의 일반 실수 대체값은 -99999.0이다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=81]` ` real(8), parameter ::  doubleval = -99999.d0 `) 정수 기본 대체값은 -99999이다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=82]` ` integer, parameter :: intFillValue = -99999 `) 핫스타트 방사 응력의 _FillValue는 0.0d0이다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=5605]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(hs%ncid, hs%rs1%u_nodal_data_id, & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=5606]` ` '_FillValue', 0.0d0) `) 변수별 예외는 아래 출력 차이 표와 netCDF 변수 목록에서 구분한다.

fort.77.nc의 weir_dz도 _FillValue에 0.0d0을 기록한다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2677]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'weir_dz' `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2697]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(dat%ncid, dat%nodal_data_id, & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2698]` ` '_FillValue', 0.0d0) `)
일반 관측소 유속 변수의 _FillValue와 dry_Value는 doubleval이다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1340]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(sta%ncid, sta%u_station_data_id, & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1341]` ` '_FillValue', doubleval) `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1343]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(sta%ncid, sta%u_station_data_id, & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1344]` ` 'dry_Value', doubleval) `)


| 항목 | 문서·코드 대조 | 출처와 원문 |
|---|---|---|
| fill과 dry 속성 fill과 dry 속성 | doubleval과 intFillValue는 -99999이다. weir_dz의 _FillValue는 0.0d0이다. 아래 변수별 표는 실제 속성 정의를 적는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort63.rst line=19]` `` k, :ref:`ETA2(k) <ETA2>` ``<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=81]` ` real(8), parameter ::  doubleval = -99999.d0 `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=82]` ` integer, parameter :: intFillValue = -99999 `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1997]` ` '_FillValue', doubleval) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2149]` ` 'dry_Value', doubleval) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2152]` ` 'dry_Value', doubleval) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3473]` ` '_FillValue', intFillValue) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=2698]` ` '_FillValue', 0.0d0) ` |

| D06 / fort.16 / GFDL 자료 설명 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=19]` ` The Geophysical Fluid Dynamics Laboratory (GFDL) file format was developed to support the output from atmospheric models. The files have the following characteristics: `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=21]` ` * Each ASCII GFDL met file contains one or more nested grid data where the nested grids are allowed to change in time. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=22]` ` * Coarse grid data is not stored where finer nest data is given. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=24]` ` The file format of the ADCIRC fort.22 for GFDL is as follows: `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=26]` ` Line 1 — ASCII Text Header `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=27]` ` Line 2 — Windmultiplier Value (real number) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=28]` ` Line 3 — Maximum Extrapolation distance (m) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort16.rst line=29]` ` Lines 4-end — cycleTime rampValue filename ` | `[file=src/logging.F90 line=343]` ` open (log_unit, FILE=trim(localdir)//'/'//'fort.16', ACTION='WRITE', STATUS='REPLACE') `<br>`[file=src/logging.F90 line=401]` ` write (log_unit, '(A)') formatLogMessage(log_level, message) ` | 문서의 이 절은 fort.22 입력 자료를 설명한다. fort.16 쓰기 형식이 아니다. |
| D07 / fort.33 / ITPACK 출력 대상 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort33.rst line=6]` ` Diagnostic output from the ITPACKV 2D solver is written to this file if the solver encounters difficulty converging. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1245]` ` ISLDIA = 0 fatal error messgs only from ITPACKV 2D(fort.33) ` | `[file=src/gwce.F line=147]` ` IPARM(2)=ISLDIA `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=149]` ` IPARM(4)=log_unit `<br>`[file=src/itpackv.F line=215]` ` level = iparm(2) `<br>`[file=src/itpackv.F line=216]` ` nout = iparm(4) `<br>`[file=src/itpackv.F line=217]` ` if (level.ge.1) write (nout,10) ` | 확인하지 않음. 코드는 ITPACK의 출력 단위에 log_unit=16을 넘긴다. fort.33을 여는 쓰기 경로를 찾지 못했다. level>=1에서는 정상 시작 진단도 단위 16에 쓴다. |

| D12 / fort.72 / 단위 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort72.rst line=30]` ` * Wind velocity components (WVNX, WVNY) are given in meters per second `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2327]` ` x,y wind stress (NWS =1, 2, -2) or velocity (NWS = 3, 4, -4, 5, -5, 6, 10, 11) at NSTAM meteorological recording stations. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, velocity is m/s, if gravity is in ft/s2, velocity is in ft/s). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the velocity units are m/s. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2343]` ` x,y wind stress (NWS =1, 2, -2) or velocity (NWS = 3, 4, -4, 5, -5, 6, 10, 11) or converted from 1-min average wind to 10-min average wind velocity (one2ten=0.8928) multiplied by the wind ramp in m/s (NWS = 8, 19, 20) for all nodes in the domain. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, velocity is m/s, if gravity is in ft/s2, velocity is in ft/s). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the velocity units are m/s. ` | `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1180]` ` 'long_name', 'station wind stress u-component') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1184]` ` 'station_eastward_surface_wind_stress') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1267]` ` if (abs(nws) > 2 .and. abs(nws) < 100) then `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1269]` ` 'm s-1', 'ft s-1') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1273]` ` call putUnitsAttribute(sta%ncid, sta%u_station_data_id, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1274]` ` 'm2 s-2', 'ft s-2') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1275]` ` call putUnitsAttribute(sta%ncid, sta%u_station_data_id, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1276]` ` 'm2 s-2', 'ft s-2') ` | 문서의 무조건 m/s 설명은 응력 출력 분기를 설명하지 못한다. | D20 / fort.47 / 생성 조건·출력 간격<br>fort.47 / 헤더 DTDP*NSPO3DGD<br>fort.47 / 헤더 NSPO3DGD<br>POST47 / 헤더·TIME/IT·수온 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=6]` `` The fort.47 file records the top temperature boundary condition that is either provided via output from an atmospheric model or as an input variable into the code via a Surface Temperature Boundary Values File. This output file follows the same format as the :doc:`Water Surface Elevation <fort63>` file, as it only records the temperature values for the surface layer. The output file is only provided if the :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` value in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <../input_files/fort15>` is given as a 3 or 4, as ADCIRC evaluates the temperature changes for these two :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` values. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort47.rst line=17]` ` * Output timing matches fort.63 parameters (NSPOOLGE) ` | `[file=src/cstart.F line=1053]` ` IF (ABS(I3DGD).EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1055]` ` IF((IDEN.EQ.3).OR.(IDEN.EQ.4)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1056]` ` IF (BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1059]` ` WRITE(47,498) NDSet3DGD,NP,DTDP*NSpo3DGD,NSpo3DGD,1 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3880]` ` IF((IDEN.EQ.3).OR.(IDEN.EQ.4)) THEN `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3881]` ` IF(BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0) THEN `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=3882]` ` OPEN(47,FILE=TRIM(LOCALDIR)//'/'//'fort.47', `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1058]` ` WRITE(47,499) RUNDES,RUNID,AGRID `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5241]` ` WRITE(47,'(A85)') INLINE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5242]` ` WRITE(47,3645) NDSET,NNODG,DT,NSPO3DGD,IRTYPE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5248]` ` IF (IPROC.EQ.1) WRITE(47,2110) TIMEOUTE,ITE `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5258]` ` CALL NEWINDEX(TOPTEMP(I),OUTMSG,I) `<br>`[file=prep/post.F line=5259]` ` WRITE(47,*) TRIM(OUTMSG) !jgf46.00 TRIM to cut down file size ` | 문서는 NSPOOLGE를 적는다. 코드는 I3DGD와 NSPO3DGD를 사용한다. IDEN은 양수 3 또는 4이어야 한다. BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0 조건도 필요하다.<br>코드는 fort.63 계열의 다섯 숫자 형태를 쓰지만 시간 제어는 3차원 밀도 출력이다.<br>합치기는 NSPO3DGD를 쓴다. 문서는 NSPOOLGE를 적는다. 수온 자료는 전역 노드 번호로 다시 쓴다. |
| D21 / fort.45·46 / IRTYPE=3 의미 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2250]` ` The record type (= 1 for elevation files, = 2 for velocity files, and = 3 for 3D velocity file) ` | `[file=src/cstart.F line=1041]` ` IF((IDen.EQ.1).OR.(IDen.EQ.0)) IRType=1 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1042]` ` IF((IDen.EQ.2).OR.(IDen.EQ.3)) IRType=2 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1043]` ` IF(IDen.EQ.4) IRType=3 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1081]` ` CALL initOutput3D(45, I3DGV, NDSET3DGV, NP, NP_G, `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1082]` ` &                  NSPO3DGV, IRType, I3DGVRec) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1085]` ` CALL initOutput3D(46, I3DGT, NDSET3DGT, NP, NP_G, `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=1086]` ` &                  NSPO3DGT, IRType, I3DGTRec) ` | cold start는 밀도 헤더 준비 후의 IRType를 재사용한다. IDEN=0·1이면 1이다. IDEN=2·3이면 2이다. 문서의 3D velocity record type 3과 다르다. |

| D25 / 조화분석 속도 진폭 단위<br>fort.51–54.nc / 진폭·위상 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2151]` ` X direction velocity amplitude for constituent k at station j. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, amplitude is in m, if gravity is in ft/s2, amplitude is in ft.). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the amplitude is in m. This quantity is computed by the harmonic analysis routines in ADCIRC. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2161]` ` Y direction velocity amplitude for constituent k at station j. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, amplitude is in m, if gravity is in ft/s2, amplitude is in ft.). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the amplitude is in m. This quantity is computed by the harmonic analysis routines in ADCIRC. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2171]` ` X direction velocity amplitude for constituent k at node or station j. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, amplitude is in m, if gravity is in ft/s2, amplitude is in ft.). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the amplitude is in m. This quantity is computed by the harmonic analysis routines in ADCIRC. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort51.rst line=21]` `` :ref:`EMAG(k,j) <EMAG>`, :ref:`PHASEDE(k,j) <PHASEDE>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort52.rst line=21]` `` :ref:`UMAG(k,j) <UMAG>`, :ref:`PHASEDU(k,j) <PHASEDU>`, :ref:`VMAG(k,j) <VMAG>`, :ref:`PHASEDV(k,j) <PHASEDV>` `` | `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1889]` ` 'm/s', 'ft/s') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1941]` ` 'm/s', 'ft/s') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=909]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(sta%ncid, 'amp', NF90_DOUBLE, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=921]` ` 'm', 'ft') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=926]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(sta%ncid, 'phs', NF90_DOUBLE, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=938]` ` 'deg', 'deg') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=949]` ` iret = nf90_def_var(sta%ncid, 'u-vel-amp', NF90_DOUBLE, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=971]` ` 'm/s', 'ft/s') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1812]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'amp' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1833]` ` 'm', 'ft') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1838]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'phs' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1859]` ` 'deg', 'deg') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1868]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'u_amp' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1894]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'u_phs' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1915]` ` 'deg', 'deg') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1920]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'v_amp' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1946]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'v_phs' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1967]` ` 'deg', 'deg') ` | 매개변수 문서는 m 또는 ft를 적는다. netCDF는 m/s 또는 ft/s를 쓴다. 속도 진폭은 속도 배열에서 계산한다.<br>수위 진폭은 m/ft이다. 유속 진폭은 m/s 또는 ft/s이다. 위상은 deg이다. 유속 진폭을 길이로 설명하는 문서는 코드와 다르다. |
| D26 / fort.55 / EAVDIF·EVADIF 의미 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2191]` ` Elevation variance in the resynthesized time series `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2201]` ` Elevation variance in the resynthesized time series ` | `[file=src/harm.F line=1351]` ` if(elav(n).eq.0.) then `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1352]` ` if(eav(n).eq.0.) eavdif(n)=1.0d0 `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1353]` ` if(eav(n).ne.0.) eavdif(n)=99d19 `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1355]` ` eavdif(n)=eav(n)/elav(n) `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1357]` ` if(elva(n).eq.0.) then `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1358]` ` if(esq(n).eq.0.) evadif(n)=1.0d0 `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1359]` ` if(esq(n).ne.0.) evadif(n)=99e19 `<br>`[file=src/harm.F line=1361]` ` evadif(n)=esq(n)/elva(n) ` | 문서는 두 값을 분산(variance)으로 설명한다. 코드는 각각 재합성 평균/모델 평균과 재합성 분산(variance)/모델 분산(variance)의 비를 쓴다. 0 분모 분기의 특수값은 인용 식 그대로이다. |

| D29 / maxwvel.63 / 값 WVNOUTMAX | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/maxwvel63.rst line=8]` ` This file records the maximum wind speed magnitude at each node throughout the simulation. The values represent the highest wind speeds experienced at each node during the model run, which is crucial for understanding the wind forcing conditions and their potential impacts on storm surge and wave generation. ` | `[file=src/write_output.F line=1348]` ` WVMaxDescript % array                 => WVNOUTMAX `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6475]` ` if ((wvnxout(i)**2+wvnyout(i)**2).gt.wvnoutmax(i)**2) then `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6476]` ` wvnoutmax(i) = sqrt(wvnxout(i)**2+wvnyout(i)**2) `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6477]` ` wvnoutmax_time(i) = timeLoc ` | 문서는 wind speed로 설명한다. NWS=1, ±2, ±7이면 WVNXOUT/WVNYOUT는 정규화 응력 성분이다(wind.F:908·917–918, 926·948–949, 1142·1164–1165). 원래 "NWS=±1·±2"로 적었다. 그 분기에서는 응력 크기의 극값이다. |
| D30 / maxrs.63 / 값 RSNMAX | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/maxrs63.rst line=8]` ` This file records the maximum wave radiation stress at each node throughout the simulation. IDEN=4이면 SAL과 TEMP를 쓴다.<br>합치기도 상태를 필드 배열별로 쓴다. 층 자료는 필드→층→노드 순서이다. 문서는 노드→층→필드 순서이다. 밀도·염분·온도 활성 조건은 아래 행을 따른다. |

| D60 / depth / English 길이 단위<br>barht·pipediam / English 길이 단위 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=58]` ` Bathymetric depth with respect to the geoid, positive below the geoid and negative above the geoid. Bathymetric depths above the geoid or sufficiently small that nodes will dry, require that the wetting/drying option is enabled (NOLIFA=2 in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File.) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=492]` ` Gravitational constant. The units of this constant determine the distance units that ADCIRC operates with. (ADCIRC always operates in seconds and therefore the time units for G must be seconds.) When ICS = 2, it is required that G = 9.81 m/sec2. Regardless of ICS, when either NTIP = 1 or NCOR = 1, it is required that G = 9.81 m/sec2. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=28]` ` Internal barrier height (positive above the geoid and negative below the geoid) at node NBVV(k,j) and the paired node IBCONN(k,j)). The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at these nodes, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j) and <b >BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(IBCONN(k,j)). If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=38]` ` External barrier height (positive above the geoid and negative below the geoid) at node NBVV(k,j). The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at this node, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARLANHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j). If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=133]` ` Cross barrier pipe diameter in internal barrier with cross barrier G 문서는 중력의 단위가 모델의 길이 단위를 정한다고 적는다. writer는 경계 높이와 관 직경을 변환 없이 복사한다. units는 m으로 고정한다. 해석: English 길이 단위의 Cartesian 실행에서는 barht·pipediam의 값과 속성이 다르다. PIPEHT의 개별 문서 docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:123 `The height of the pipe above the geoid (m) at the j-th node in the k-th normal flow specified boundary segment. 않는다.<br>C3D 경로는 미지원 메시지를 쓴다. 2차원 전용 binary writer는 카운터 뒤에 3차원·조화 상태를 쓰는 구간이 없다. 직접 writeHotstart의 전체 구조와 구분해야 한다.<br>합치기는 조화 상태를 읽는 구간을 갖는다. 전체 쓰기 구간 3973–4155는 2차원·카운터·3차원 상태 뒤에 파일을 닫는다. 그 쓰기 구간은 조화 행렬·load vector·평균 배열을 다시 쓰지 않는다. 문서의 조화 상태 포함 구조와 다르다. |
| D64 / windDrag.173 / 이름·조건·간격 | `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=142]` ` In all cases, the actual wind drag coefficients determined by ADCIRC can be `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=143]` `` output to a :ref:`fort.63 <fort63>`-type file named ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=144]` ``` ``winddrag.173``. Output settings (file format, output start/end ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=145]` ` times, and output interval) match those of either the fort.63 or fort.73/74 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=146]` ` files. Outputting of this file is enabled by setting `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=147]` ``` ``outputWindDrag=.TRUE.`` in the ``&metControl`` namelist of the :ref:`fort.15 file's ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst line=148]` `` namelist section <fort15>`. `` | `[file=src/write_output.F line=955]` ` windDragDescript % lun                  = 173 `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=956]` ` windDragDescript % specifier            = NOUTGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=957]` ` windDragDescript % startTimeStep           = NTCYSGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=958]` ` windDragDescript % endTimeStep             = NTCYFGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=959]` ` windDragDescript % outputTimeStepIncrement = NSPOOLGW `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=963]` ` windDragDescript % field_name           = 'windDrag' `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=964]` ` windDragDescript % file_basename        = 'windDrag' `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=966]` ` windDragDescript % writeFlag            = outputWindDrag ` | 문서는 winddrag.173을 적는다. 코드는 windDrag를 basename으로 쓴다. 문서는 fort.63 또는 fort.73/74 설정을 허용한다. 이 descriptor는 NOUTGW와 전역 기상 출력 창·간격만 연결한다. 활성 조건 outputWindDrag는 같다. |

| D66 / fort.41.nc·fort.44.nc / G의 otherwise 단위 속성 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2704]` ` Sigma T value (kg/m^3) (=density-1000) at a specified recording station `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2709]` ` Salinity (PSU) at a specified recording station `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2714]` ` Temperature (DEG C) at a specified recording station ` | `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=700]` ` "kg/m^3", "n/a") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=715]` ` "PSU", "n/a") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=732]` ` "Celsius", "Fahrenheit") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1570]` ` "kg/m^3", "n/a") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1582]` ` "PSU", "PSU") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=1595]` ` "Celsius", "Fahrenheit") `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10455]` ` if (G < 11.d0) then `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10456]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(ncid, var_id, 'units', metric) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10457]` ` else `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10458]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(ncid, var_id, 'units', english) ` | 문서는 SIGTSTA를 kg/m^3로 적는다. SALSTA를 PSU로 적는다. TEMPSTA를 DEG C로 적는다. G<11.d0이면 해당 속성이 맞는다. otherwise 분기는 관측소 SIGT·SAL에 n/a를 쓴다. 관측소·전역 TEMP에 Fahrenheit를 쓴다. 전역 SIGT에도 n/a를 쓴다. 전역 SAL의 PSU는 같다. 이는 출력 단위 속성의 차이이다. 실제 온도 값의 변환 정확도는 실행하지 않았다. |
| D67 / maxinundepth.63.nc / inun_max의 English 단위 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/maxinundepth63.rst line=4]` `` The maximum inundation depth (maxinundepth.63) file records the peak inundation depth (in meters) above ground that occurred during the simulation. The data are only recorded in areas that are initially dry according to the :doc:`initiallydry.63 <initiallydry63>` file. The values are -99999.0 if the area was never wet during the simulation. `` | `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3394]` ` case (401) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3395]` ` dat%varnames(1) = 'inun_max' `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3437]` ` call putUnitsAttribute(dat%ncid, dat%max_nodal_data_id, & `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3438]` ` 'm', 'ft') `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10455]` ` if (G < 11.d0) then `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10456]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(ncid, var_id, 'units', metric) `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10457]` ` else `<br>`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=10458]` ` iret = nf90_put_att(ncid, var_id, 'units', english) `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6372]` ` if ((eta2(i)+dp(i)).gt.maxInunDepth(i)) then `<br>`[file=src/write_output.F line=6373]` ` maxInunDepth(i) = eta2(i) + dp(i) ` | 문서는 peak inundation depth를 in meters로 한정한다. G<11.d0 분기는 units=m을 쓴다. otherwise 분기는 units=ft를 쓴다. 배열 값은 ETA2+DP에서 얻는다. 이 판정은 문서와 출력 단위 속성의 차이이다. |

## References

- `src/write_output.F` — main writers (init + writeOutput2D/3D + harmonic + minmax).
- `src/globalio.F` — header functions per output type.
- `src/timestep.F` — `collectMinMaxData`, `updateHarmonicAnalysis` per-step calls.
- `src/harm.F` — accumulator + solve.
- `src/adcirc.F` — end-of-run writeOutHarmonic dispatch.
- `prep/post.F` — POST61-74 merge routines.
